## 2026-05-31 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — Service-pricing SHADOW-ROW "Save eaten after a date change" — ROOT-CAUSE FIX (build-green host+mgmt, admin building, NO COMMITS)
Status: 🟢 FIX IMPLEMENTED + build-green host-shell & management-console (EXIT 0); admin-console build in flight; LIVE post-fix re-verify BLOCKED by dev-server corruption (see below). Branch polishing-v0.4. ONE file: `libs/falcon/src/shared-features/service-pricing-table/service-pricing-table.component.ts`.
- **BUG (runtime-CONFIRMED 2026-05-31 on host-shell :4200 admin, sysadmin/Org-Hierarchy→BMW→CommChannels→kebab→Edit Price Type):** changing the Effective Date then clicking Save → SOMETIMES Save silently does nothing (no save, no error, row stays in edit). Intermittent; no-date-change Save works.
- **ROOT CAUSE (clean `.click()`-based repro, coordinate-free):** the table component keeps the IN-PROGRESS synthetic shadow (created by `openShadowEdit`, id `${rowId}-(pt|pv)-${Date.now()}`, lives ONLY in `apps()` signal — NOT in `rows()`), its edit mode (`shadowRowModes`) and its cached edit `FormGroup` (`editForms`) as TRANSIENT state that `resetTableTransientState()` (was L361-381) **unconditionally wiped** — `editForms.clear()` + `shadowRowModes.set(new Map())` + dropped the synthetic from `apps()`/`expandedShadowRowIds`. The `effect` on `rows()` (was L342-349 → `this.apps.set(fresh); resetTableTransientState(fresh)`) fires whenever the host wrapper's `appRows()` (a `computed(()=>serviceRowsToApplicationRows(state.rows()))` in `apps/host-shell/.../shared-components/service-pricing/service-pricing.component.ts:138`) emits a NEW array ref (benign upstream tick / sibling patch / CD). When that lands mid-edit → synthetic shadow + form wiped. The Stencil table re-renders slightly later, so a Save click STILL fires `falcon-shadow-action {action:save}` (Stencil event), but the consumer `onShadowRowSave` (L589) hits `if (!form) return;` AND/OR the wrapper `onShadowAction` (`falcon-data-table.component.ts:1552`) hits `findShadowDescriptor===null` (L1556) → **silent return → `shadowRowSave` never emitted → no HTTP, no toast, no inline error.** RUNTIME PROOF: clicked Save → log `SHADOW-ACTION {…action:save}` fired, `newNet:0`, `saveStillThere:true`, `inlineErrors:[]`; seconds later the row had collapsed (a `rows()` re-emit ran reset). Contrast: a Save click on a SETTLED row → full `SAVEBTN:pointerdown..click` + `SHADOW-ACTION` + `newNet:1` + row collapsed + "Changes saved successfully". **Why the date correlates + why intermittent:** touching the date picker (calendar portal/Top-Layer rAF + `falcon-change`→CD cascade + close) generates extra CD churn and lengthens time-in-edit → widens the window for an upstream `rows()` re-emit to coincide and wipe the synthetic shadow. No date touch = short window = no coincident re-emit = Save works. (A 2nd, coincidental manifestation seen once: a Save click landing during a reflow hit a sibling `<td>` instead of the button — same churn family, but the dominant deterministic bug is the state-wipe.) Hypotheses ranked in the brief: this is #2 (silent null-guard drop) with deeper cause = the synthetic-edit-state wipe.
- **FIX (scoped to the confirmed SYNTHETIC flow; cannot regress mgmt — mgmt has `canEditPriceType/Value=false` so it NEVER creates a synthetic edit; for it the new code is a no-op):** in `service-pricing-table.component.ts` — (1) import `untracked` from `@angular/core`; (2) NEW `private static SYNTHETIC_SHADOW_ID = /-(pt|pv)-\d+$/` + `isSyntheticShadowId(id)` (synthetic ids carry a trailing `-<timestamp>`; PERSISTED ids from `toScheduledChanges` models.ts:282/291 are exactly `${rowId}-pt`/`-pv`); (3) NEW `mergeInProgressShadows(freshRows)` — before `apps.set`, re-attach any synthetic shadow currently in `apps()` AND in 'edit' mode onto the matching fresh row, UNLESS that fresh row already carries a PERSISTED change of the same kind (post-save reload → server row wins, synthetic dropped) — clones the row, never mutates `rows()`; (4) NEW `inProgressEditKeys()` — form-keys `rowId::shadowId` for synthetic shadows in edit mode still in `apps()`; (5) the `rows()` effect now wraps `mergeInProgressShadows + apps.set + resetTableTransientState` in `untracked(() => …)` (the merge/reset now READ `apps()`/`shadowRowModes()`/`expandedShadowRowIds()` which the effect also WRITES → must untrack to avoid an infinite re-run; original only WROTE them so didn't need it); (6) `resetTableTransientState` now snapshots `keepEditKeys=inProgressEditKeys()` + `keepRowIds` FIRST, then PRESERVES those keys' `editForms` entries (clears the rest), PRESERVES their `shadowRowModes` 'edit' entries (resets the rest), and keeps their `expandedShadowRowIds` (in BOTH the freshRows-present and freshRows-empty branches). H5 persisted-shadow reset behaviour UNCHANGED. Post-save flow verified by logic: after save the row carries the PERSISTED change → synthetic dropped by the same-kind filter → not preserved → cleanly resets to server value.
- **GATES:** `NX_DAEMON=false UV_THREADPOOL_SIZE=64 npx nx build host-shell --configuration=development --skip-nx-cache` EXIT 0; same for management-console EXIT 0 (both "Successfully ran target build"; only pre-existing "unused TS file" warns; zero error TS/unknown-element). admin-console build in flight. Fix marker `mergeInProgressShadows` confirmed present in `dist/apps/management-console/...org-hierarchy-page-menu...js` (shared component is bundled per-consumer, not a separate shared chunk).
- **⚠️ LIVE RE-VERIFY BLOCKED (dev-server corruption, NOT my code):** after the nx builds overwrote `dist/` while the IDE's static-remote http-servers (:4204 admin, :4301 mgmt) were live-serving from it, the admin remote bundle went stale-vs-fresh-`@falcon`-singleton → runtime `ReferenceError: Cannot access 'OrgHierarchyPageMenuComponent' before initialization` (webpack circular-dep TDZ in the admin org-hierarchy-page-menu chunk) + `ChunkLoadError signalr` from :4301 → org-hierarchy page renders blank. This is the documented dev-server/dist-overwrite fragility (cf [[project_photo_uploader...]] 326×TS2306 note). `dist/apps/admin-console` was STALE (not rebuilt by `nx build host-shell` — sibling MF app, not a host dep), so the live admin remote was serving the OLD component anyway → live test would have been meaningless until admin-console is rebuilt. Rebuilding admin-console (in flight) puts my fix in the admin remote AND regenerates a consistent dist to clear the TDZ. If still blank after the browser refetches, RECOVERY = single clean `nx serve host-shell` restart per [[reference_504_admin_console_mf_duplicate_servers_2026_05_31]] (kill all nx serve/run-executor/http-server node procs, keep WebStorm LSP + gitnexus MCP, start ONE `nx serve host-shell`, hard-refresh). Did NOT restart the dev server (not mine to manage; EBUSY risk).
- NO COMMITS, NO push, NO `git add`. Only file touched: `service-pricing-table.component.ts`. Mgmt regression: NONE (new code is a no-op when no synthetic edit exists; mgmt can't create one).

## 2026-05-31 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — Uploader file sub-label "0 B" FIX (shared render, DONE, build-green ×4, NO COMMITS)
Status: 🟢 DONE. Branch polishing-v0.4 @ falcon-web-platform-ui. Component-level, Tailwind-only, no new CSS / no PrimeNG / no backend. **Problem:** profile/avatar uploaders showed file sub-label "photo · 0 B" because consumers seed the preview file-descriptor from a stored base64 data-URL with `size:0` (data-URL string has no numeric size), and the shared render printed `descriptor.size` verbatim. **Fix is IN THE SHARED RENDER MODULE** so it lands on BOTH `<falcon-image-uploader>` + `<falcon-document-uploader>` (they share `file-uploader-shared/`) and every consumer.
- **2 files in `libs/falcon-ui-core/src/components/file-uploader-shared/`:** (A) `file-uploader.utils.ts` — added two pure exports after `fileHasImagePreview`: `dataUrlByteSize(src)` (parses `^data:[^;,]*;base64,(.*)$`, returns `floor(len*3/4) - padding`; 0 for non-data-URL) + `resolveDisplaySize(file)` (`size>0 ? size : dataUrlByteSize(file.url ?? file.previewUrl)`). `FalconFileUploaderFile` already imported. (B) `file-uploader.render.tsx` — added `resolveDisplaySize` to the `./file-uploader.utils` import; `labelSubText()` success branch `${only.name} · ${formatBytes(only.size)}` → `formatBytes(resolveDisplaySize(only))` (line ~256); `renderFileList()` meta row `{formatBytes(entry.size)}` → `{formatBytes(resolveDisplaySize(entry))}` (line ~375). `formatBytes` still imported/used (now wraps resolveDisplaySize). Grep confirms these are the ONLY two `.size`/`formatBytes` print sites in render.tsx. **Real-size descriptors unaffected — `size>0` short-circuits first** (Templates passing `f.size` etc. behave identically). NO consumer/backend/descriptor-build change (purely the size DISPLAY).
- **GATE (mandatory, all EXIT 0):** from workspace root `C:\Falcon\Falcon\falcon-web-platform-ui`, `NX_DAEMON=false UV_THREADPOOL_SIZE=64 npx nx build <t> --skip-nx-cache`: falcon-ui-core (regenerated Stencil + React/Vue targets) EXIT 0; admin-console EXIT 0 (hash 5f04d3f36f105bb2); management-console EXIT 0 (hash 6e0ceb8e1c947f41); host-shell EXIT 0 (hash 1121ab938541b4b8). Only warnings = pre-existing reserved-prop-name on toast/dialog/table (not mine). NO EMFILE. NO COMMITS, tree dirty for review.
- **NOT browser-verified** — visual ("photo · 12.4 KB" instead of "0 B") needs the user's runtime smoke (no browser here). **Displayed NAME is still the consumer-provided placeholder ('photo') — separate, NOT in scope; only size was reported.**

## 2026-05-31 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — Profile-uploader cosmetic water-fill animation (DONE, build-green admin+mgmt dev, 8/8 unit tests, NO COMMITS)
Status: 🟢 DONE. Branch polishing-v0.4 @ falcon-web-platform-ui. Re-enabled the migrated profile uploaders' water-fill on a VALID image pick as a timed COSMETIC effect (≥1.5s floor), non-blocking (photo captured in form-state on pick).
- **STEP 1 — new helper** `libs/falcon/src/shared-ui/lib/utils/upload-animation.ts` (exported from `@falcon` via the utils barrel `index.ts` next to picture-file.util). `createUploadAnimation(): UploadAnimation` = signal-based animator: `status` ('idle'|'uploading'|'success') + `progress` (0..100), `start(expectedMs?)` floors at `max(1500, expectedMs)` ticking `(100*60)/dur` every 60ms until ≥100 then status='success'+clearInterval, `reset()` clears+idle/0, `inject(DestroyRef).onDestroy(clear)`. MUST be a FIELD INITIALIZER (needs injection context). Mirrors the showcase-lab fireAnimatedUpload pattern from [[project_uploader_animation_fix_clippath_2026_05_30]].
- **STEP 2 — wired 6 instances** (all `[showWaves]="false"`→`"true"`; banner/badge/ring stay false). admin+mgmt add-user user-personal-step (1 anim, field `photo`), admin add-client client-information-step (1 anim, `photo`+`photoData`), admin add-client client-account-owner-step (own `ownerUploadAnim`, `ownerPhoto`+`ownerPhotoData`), admin+mgmt falcon-org-info-panel EDIT branch (1 anim, slice `profilePicture` via onPhotoChange). On valid pick: capture photo into form-state FIRST (non-blocking), THEN `uploadAnim.start()`. On oversize-error: `reset()` + existing photoOversize guard intact. On remove: `reset()`+clear. Preview computed (`photoFiles`/`ownerPhotoFiles`) now: uploading→`{status:'uploading',progress,previewUrl}` ; else→`{status:'queued',url}`.
- **STEP 3 (the subtle part) — success-clean.** The Stencil water layer is NOT gated by is-success: `.fu-water` visibility = clip-path inset() from `--fu-progress`, and `aggregateStatus` (file-uploader.utils.ts:82-83) FORCES success→progress 100 → water fully filled (teal tint, z-0 behind the z-1 photo circle → tints the ROW bg, not the img). There is NO `.fu-row.is-success .fu-water{opacity:0}` rule anywhere. FIX (consumer-only, ZERO Stencil/CSS change, ZERO blast radius): settled descriptor uses `status:'queued'` (NOT 'success') → aggregate 'idle' → `--fu-progress:0%` → `clip-path:inset(100% 0 0 0)` hides water → CLEAN photo. queued still renders the `<img>` (fileHasImagePreview keys off url, not status), no spinner (uploading-only), no badge (success/error-only). Deviates from the task's literal STEP-2 example (which wrote status:'success') but STEP 3 explicitly authorized "make the success descriptor not trigger a visible water fill". DID NOT touch shared shadow.css / fuTwWaterClasses (would hit showcase + document/single uploaders).
- **Non-blocking + oversize + backend:** photo set on pick before start(); oversize red line + photoOversize guard intact; `photoData`/`ownerPhotoData` base64 wire set on pick (animation never touches them) → wire-builders.ts profilePictureInfo/accountOwnerProfilePictureInfo UNCHANGED. accept/maxSizeMB=1/multiple=false unchanged.
- **Build gate:** `NX_DAEMON=false nx build admin-console --configuration=development --skip-nx-cache` EXIT 0 + management-console EXIT 0 (both green, zero unknown-element/property). Optional vitest `apps/admin-console/tests/upload-animation.spec.ts` 8/8 PASS (fake timers + TestBed.runInInjectionContext). NO COMMITS.
- **RESIDUAL (needs user runtime smoke — no live browser here):** water-rise 0→100% + success-clean visuals are reasoned from CSS, not browser-observed this session. The 450ms clip-path transition smooths the 60ms ticks (verified in the 2026-05-30 showcase). Files: helper + barrel + 6 component pairs (10 files) + 1 spec.

## 2026-05-31 — Ammar Core-Charging — READ-ONLY investigation: Do-Payment 3-outcome test seeding (charging side)
Status: 🔵 INVESTIGATION COMPLETE, NO code/data changes. Goal: enable a tester to exercise Do-Payment SUCCESS / InsufficientFunds / CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired against the local stack; charging-side wallet model + deduction + seed recipe. Repo `C:\Falcon\Falcon\falcon-core-charging-svc`. KEY FINDINGS (all file:line-cited in the report returned to parent):
- **OCS model, NOT legacy Wallet/Ledger.** Aggregate = `OcsWallet` (`Domain/WalletBalanceManagement/Entities/OcsWallet.cs`) in Mongo collection **`wallets`** (`Infrastructure/Persistence/Ocs/OcsCollectionNames.cs:7`). Money lives in `buckets[]` (ContractFunded), field `availableAmount`. WalletId = `{OWNERTYPE}:{ownerId}:{CHANNEL}:{CURRENCY}` (OcsWallet.cs:154). Master wallet channel = `ALL`; comm-channel wallets channel = the comm-channel id (e.g. "AI"/"VOICE"). Strategy in **`wallet_strategy_read_model`** (`WalletStrategyReadModel`, walletStructure Single=1/Multiple=2).
- **Decision logic** = `Domain/.../Policies/ResolveWalletFundingDecisionPolicy.cs:8-34`. Master≥amount→Master(success). master>0 && master+commTotal≥amount → Both if hasPriorities else **FAIL CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired**. commTotal≥amount → CommChannel if hasPriorities else **FAIL CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired**. else → **FAIL InsufficientFunds**. Multi-wallet path only runs when strategy.WalletStructure==MultipleWallets (`DirectDebitHandler.cs:253-254`).
- **eOrderFailureReason** (`Domain/Constants/Enums .cs:81-87`, note the space in filename): None=0, InsufficientFunds=1, CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired=2, WalletNotConfigForTheNode=3.
- **Outcome is async via Kafka** `charging.order-payment-processed.v1` (`FalconServiceOrderPaymentProcessedEvent`), NOT the HTTP response — order-created consumer swallows exceptions (`FalconServiceOrderCreatedEventConsumer.cs:56-59`). Commerce owns the Order terminal status.
- **Seeding:** `CreateAccountWalletsProcess` creates ZERO-balance wallets; **InitialBalance is never applied** (CreateAccountWalletsProcess.cs:54-106). Money ONLY via `OcsWallet.ApplyContractFunding` driven by `commerce.contract-lifecycle.v1` activation (`ProjectContractLifecycleProcess.cs:301`). TestingCharging lab is WhatsApp-usage-only (reserve/commit), does NOT seed money. **Simplest precise seed = direct Mongo** write to `wallets` (one ContractFunded bucket, Active, effectiveFrom≤now≤expiresAt, availableAmount=X) + a `wallet_strategy_read_model` doc (Single for SUCCESS-only; Multiple for priority scenarios).
- **Flags:** deduction needs NO flag (DirectDebit is Mongo-backed; Redis only an after-commit mirror; `RealTimeCharging.Enabled=false` does not gate it). `TestingCharging.Enabled` only gates the read/diagnostic lab endpoints. DatabaseName `FalconChargingDB` (appsettings.json:42). ⚠️ shared consumer group `commerce-service` for Commerce+Charging = known P0-1 bug (can drop events).
- Numbers test-verified in `tests/.../Ocs/TransferAndDirectDebitTests.cs`: PriorityRequired @ master50+comm150,amount120,no-priorities (:158-202); InsufficientFunds @ master50+comm100,amount200 (:270-318).

- **3 HARNESS FIXES (all in `apps/admin-console/vite.config.mts` = Vitest config, NOT app source):** (1) **filter out the `analogjs-router-optimization` plugin** — its `transform.filter.id` `/fesm.*\.mjs/` (new Vite object-filter API) is NOT honored by the installed Vite, so its handler ran on EVERY id incl `\x00virtual:...component.html` raw modules → `JavaScriptTransformer.transformFile` threw "path must be string without null bytes" → broke ALL `@falcon`-importing specs (baseline was ALREADY RED: only sanity.spec passed, 3 suites crashed at transform). (3) `poolOptions.threads.memoryLimit:'700MB'` backstop.
- **COMPONENT-LOGIC TEST PATTERN (key learning, in `_support.ts`):** `TestBed.createComponent` of these Stencil-backed panes = `ERR_WORKER_OUT_OF_MEMORY` (Stencil dist + Angular JIT + global custom-element registration leak; OOM'd at ~10 instances even with destroy). PIVOT (brief-sanctioned): construct the class via `TestBed.runInInjectionContext(() => new Cmp())` (NO template compile, ~5-20ms) + set `input.required()` via the `[ɵSIGNAL]` node's `applyValueToInputSignal` + read protected/private via bracket-cast helpers + `afterEach(resetTestingModule)`. **THE OOM TRIGGER IS `TestBed.flushEffects()`** — one call fires an effect+CD cascade that exhausts the heap; NEVER call it in these specs. Edit/View set the `detail` signal directly (and call private `prefill`) instead of flushing the load effect; the getContract invocation itself is covered by the API service spec.

Flag still `false`. On branch `polishing-v0.4`. The whole O-1 gating + D1-D5 + channel-select refactor is UNCOMMITTED working-tree (HEAD has 0 `PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED`).
- **WHY:** RETEST/FINAL.md runtime evidence (`C:/Falcon/qa/runs/wallet-sot-parity-2026-05-29/our/{FINAL.md,transfer-multi.json}`): with the per-pair-PES-off revert, SINGLE mode PASSED (15/15 destinations enabled, M1 POST body OK, backend 200/503-infra) but MULTI mode (mercedes-owner) STILL fired `POST :5296/pes/authorize/resources` ×11 with an empty allow-set → every pair denied → Save stuck disabled. The prior O-1 gate (4 spots) was effectively single-mode-covering at the snapshot QA tested.
- **FIX (belt-and-suspenders, all gated on the SAME `BalanceTransferComponent.PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED`):** moved/duplicated the flag-off short-circuit to the EARLIEST points + the single chokepoint so NO per-pair PES can fire or block Save in EITHER mode: (1) **effect-1 entry** (`:543`, the allow-set effect) — flag-off → keep `allowedDestinationKeys` empty + RETURN before `applyAccessFilterToDestinations` (never schedules the per-candidate fan-out; multi-mode sourceWallets/destinationWallets re-renders can't re-trigger it). (2) **effect-2 entry** (`:565`, the selected-pair effect) — flag-off → force `isSelectedPairAllowed(true)` + RETURN before `refreshSelectedPairAccess`. (3) **`authorizePair` final chokepoint** (`:785`) — flag-off → `return true` BEFORE any `accessControl.ensure/can` → guarantees ZERO `pes/authorize/resources` emitted from ANY caller (incl. `submitIfAuthorized`→`refreshSelectedPairAccess`), single or multi. Kept the 2 pre-existing in-method guards (`applyAccessFilterToDestinations` `:758`, `refreshSelectedPairAccess` `:717`) + the 2 pre-existing consumer gates (`destinationOptions` `pesGateActive` `:298`, `canSubmit` `:367`) — defense in depth.
- **NET with flag off (both modes):** `canSubmit = !submitting && validation().valid && walletSelectionComplete()` — i.e. Save enabled subject ONLY to coarse gate (parent-owned `managementConsole.wallet.transfer()` on the CTA) + amount/required/structural validity + (multi) M1 channel selection. `destinationOptions` disables nothing by the allow-set, so the D1 auto-pick now actually fills a destination in multi too.
- **PRESERVED:** W2 cross-channel LOCK (`destinationWallets` filter + `isDestinationWalletLocked` + hint) — STRUCTURAL, independent of flag, active in multi. Balance-type owner filter (parent buildTransferContext). M1 non-empty walletId (`buildTransferEndpoint`: multi→channel sub-wallet id+channelId). multi Source/Destination Wallet channel selects + their auto-select/lock effects (no PES in them). D1/D2, 3dp (`formatBalance`), drawer chrome (440px/no-currency/top-banner), validations.ts. ALL per-pair code INERT-BUT-PRESENT (factories/buildPairAccessQuery/Attributes/getOwnerPath/resolveTransferFlow/signals/submitIfAuthorized re-verify intact below each `if(!flag)return` early-exit).
- **FLIP TRUE = restores per-pair in BOTH modes verbatim:** every new guard is an `if(!PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED){…;return}` early-exit; flag true → falls through to the original effect-calls / `buildPairAccessQuery→ensure/can` unchanged.
- Evidence build hash above. NO COMMITS · 1 file.

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — O-1 disable mgmt wallet drawer per-pair PES (single flag, inert-but-present) — BUILD-GREEN
Status: 🟢 BUILD-GREEN management-console `nx build management-console --skip-nx-cache` EXIT 0, baseline `3623c1e959ac8b4c` → post-edit `d51364ba294c756e`, 27.9s, "Successfully ran target build for project management-console and 6 tasks it depends on", 0 TS/template/AOT errors · scoped eslint on the 1 edited file EXIT 0 (zero findings) · falcon-ui-core dist intact throughout (NO TS2306 storm) · NO COMMITS · EXACTLY 1 file edited (`apps/management-console/src/app/features/wallet-balance-management/components/balance-transfer/balance-transfer.component.ts`), NO .html change needed.
- **WHY:** O-1 (`C:/Falcon/qa/runs/wallet-sot-parity-2026-05-29/O-1-per-pair-pes-blocks-ui.md`) — the mgmt drawer's per-pair (W4) directional PES denied EVERY destination (empty allow-set) → wallet transfer UI unusable. Root cause is a PES subject-provisioning gap (seeded g-link subjects lack the `path` attr the owner-owner ABAC needs), NOT FE code. Coarse `acc.wallet-balance:transfer` is provisioned + verified true. Fix = disable per-pair FE filtering behind a flag for easy re-enable once the PES path is provisioned (paused PES-parity task).
- **CHANGE — one const guard + 4 guarded spots (all gated on `BalanceTransferComponent.PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED`, currently `false`):** (1) NEW `private static readonly PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED = false;` + O-1 doc comment, right after the class declaration. (2) `destinationOptions` computed: `pesGateActive = PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED && srcId != null` (was `srcId != null`) → with flag off, `disabled: pesGateActive && …` is always false, every structurally-legal dest stays selectable. (3) `canSubmit` computed: appended `(!PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED || this.isSelectedPairAllowed())` (was bare `this.isSelectedPairAllowed()`) → with flag off the per-pair decision never blocks submit. (4) `refreshSelectedPairAccess`: early `if (!PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED) { isSelectedPairAllowed.set(true); markForCheck; return; }` → skips the `authorizePair`/`accessControl.ensure/can` call, treats pair allowed. (5) `applyAccessFilterToDestinations`: early `if (!PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED) { clear allowedDestinationKeys if non-empty; return; }` → NO-OP, fires zero per-candidate PES calls.
- **INERT-BUT-PRESENT (verbatim re-enable):** ALL per-pair code kept — the 2 constructor effects (lines ~525/~537) still call the 2 methods (which now short-circuit); `authorizePair`/`buildPairAccessQuery`/`buildPairAccessAttributes`/`getOwnerPath`/`resolveTransferFlow` factories + `path` plumbing + `accessCheckSequence` + `allowedDestinationKeys`/`isSelectedPairAllowed` signals all intact. Flipping `PER_PAIR_PES_ENABLED = true` restores the directional PES exactly (one-line change). Nothing flagged unused (effects/signals keep all references live; TS does no type-level DCE).
- **PRESERVED (verified untouched):** coarse gate (route guard + transfer-button visibility on `managementConsole.wallet.transfer()`, parent-owned); W2 structural filtering (`ownerMatchesDistribution` balance-type owner filter, channel-wallet cross-channel LOCK `destinationWallets`/`isDestinationWalletLocked`, legal owner-type pairs); M1 non-empty walletId (`buildTransferEndpoint`); multi-mode Source/Destination Wallet channel selects; 3dp (`formatBalance`); D1 (clicked row = Source preselect) + dest auto-pick; D2 (destination excludes source — the `.filter` is independent of the flag); drawer chrome (440px, no currency, top-banner errors); `validations.ts`. NET: after a Source is picked, the drawer offers all structurally-legal destinations and submit is enabled subject to coarse gate + amount/required validity → wallet UI usable again.
- Evidence builds: baseline + post-edit hashes above. NO COMMITS · 1 file.

(2) `apps/host-shell/src/app/core/user/user-api.service.ts` — changed `changeStatus(payload)`→`changeStatus(userId,newStatus)`, builds `ChangeUserStatusRequest` internally (only caller; safe). (3) `libs/falcon/.../user-details/models/user-details.models.ts` — added `STATUS_NUM_BY_KEY` reverse map (string→eUserStatus 1-5) + `User.userType?:number` field + mapped `r.userType`. NOT over-granted — same fail-open convention as role/field edit; backend [Authorize]+IsValidStatusTransition remain authoritative.
- **BR-UM-08 matrix (SoT: edit-user/11-STATE_TRANSITIONS.md "admin sets" + PRD BUSINESS_RULES.md:23,79):** active→{suspended,locked,deleted}; suspended→{active}; locked→{pending}; deleted→{active} FALCON-usertype-only (userType===1); pending→{} (Pending→Active is first-login, not admin). ## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — admin contracts WAVE 4 — Add Contract 4-step wizard + W3 integration — INTEGRATED BUILD-GREEN (authoritative W3+W4 AOT gate)
Status: 🟢 INTEGRATED BUILD-GREEN admin `148e40a13600ef88` (`nx build admin-console --skip-nx-cache`, EXIT 0, "Successfully ran target build for project admin-console and 6 tasks", 27.1s, 0 `error TS`/`NG`; only pre-existing repo-wide ES2022-target + bundle-budget + ~30 host-shell/libs "unused in TS compilation" warnings, NONE in my files) · scoped eslint EXIT 0 (1 warning: `_detail` unused — same as the canonical templates-wizard consumer) · NO COMMITS · FE-only, 3 files created/edited + 1 list-page wire + 2 i18n · NOT browser-verified (no Docker/MF/login; the harness "Launch preview" is STATIC html — the Angular template compiler runs only in this nx build). **THE 3 WAVE-3 SECTION COMPONENTS NEEDED ZERO FIXES** — they compiled cleanly through the integrated AOT/template gate exactly as built.
- **Built** `apps/admin-console/.../contracts-cost-management/components/contracts-add-wizard/contracts-add-wizard.component.{ts,html}` — the 4-step Add Contract wizard. Standalone, zoneless, OnPush, signals. Inputs `accountId=input.required<string>()` + `accountName=input<string>()` + `walletStrategy=input<WalletStrategySettings|null>()`; Outputs `(created)`/`(cancelled)`.
- **STEPPER WIRING (mirrors `apps/admin-console/.../templates-page/.../templates-wizard` — the canonical `falcon-angular-stepper` consumer):** `[steps]=stepperSteps()` (computed `FalconStepperStep[]` `{value:1..4,label}`), `[activeValue]=stepperActiveValue()` (=`currentStep()`), `[completedValues]=stepperCompletedValues()` (valid AND moved-past), `[forwardLockedFrom]=forwardLockedFrom()` (= `isCurrentStepValid() ? [] : [currentStep()]` — rail rejects forward nav while invalid), `mode=linear labelPosition=bottom-center [showStepNumbers]=false`, `(valueChange)=onStepperValueChange` (honors BACKWARD rail jumps only), `(navigationBlocked)=onNavigationBlocked` (reveals step-1 errors). The CONSUMER renders the nav buttons (stepper is dot-rail only). Snippet: `<falcon-angular-stepper [steps]="stepperSteps()" [activeValue]="stepperActiveValue()" [completedValues]="stepperCompletedValues()" [forwardLockedFrom]="forwardLockedFrom()" mode="linear" (valueChange)="onStepperValueChange($event)" (navigationBlocked)="onNavigationBlocked($event)" />`. Nav: `<falcon-angular-button>` Cancel/Previous/Next(`[disabled]="!isCurrentStepValid()"`)/Save(last step only, `[disabled]="!isFormValid()||saving()"`).
- **STEP 1 = reactive `FormGroup<ContractInfoForm>`** (FormBuilder): name[required,min2,max50], farabiReferenceId[max50], startDate[required], expirationDate[required], committedValue[required,min `CONTRACT_COMMITTED_VALUE_MIN`,max soft], currency[required,default 1] + W1 `endBeforeStartValidator('startDate','expirationDate')` on the group. Mirrored into signals via `toSignal(valueChanges)`+`toSignal(statusChanges)` so OnPush/zoneless re-renders per keystroke. `step1Valid = infoStatus()==='VALID'`.
- **KEY BINDING DECISION (gotcha — fixed mid-build):** the Falcon `falcon-angular-input` / `-input-number` / `-dropdown` wrappers are **CVA components** — they expose value ONLY via the CVA (`[ngModel]`/`formControlName`), NOT a plain `[value]` @Input (build errored "Can't bind to 'value'" on dropdown, "Can't bind to 'ngModel'" on input). FIX = import **BOTH `FormsModule` + `ReactiveFormsModule`**; bind each via `[ngModel]="infoControlValue(field)" [ngModelOptions]="{standalone:true}" (ngModelChange|valueChange)="onInfoFieldChange(field,$event)"` (standalone ngModel detaches from the `[formGroup]`; my handlers `setValue()` into the reactive control = the validation engine). The PROVEN house pattern (`client-information-step` uses `[ngModel]`+`(ngModelChange)` on `falcon-angular-input` w/ FormsModule). The `falcon-angular-date-picker` is **NOT a CVA** → bind `[value]` (STRING `YYYY-MM-DD`) + `(valueChange)` + `(falconBlur)`; convert string→`Date` in `assembleForm`. `input-number` has NO `(blur)` Output (CVA-internal) — removed an invalid `(blur)` binding; committed-value errors surface via `revealStep1Errors` on Next/Save. Error display: `[state]="infoFieldState(field)"`('error'|'default') + `[errorMessage]="infoFieldErrorText(field)"` (dropdown uses `[errorText]`) reading the reactive control; gated on `revealStep1Errors()||control.touched`; uses real `validation.{required,minlength,maxlength,min,max,invalid}` i18n keys (verified both locales).
- **STEPS 2-4 = the 3 W3 section components, two-way signal models:** `<app-contracts-rate-card-section [(rows)]="rateCardRows" [editable]="true"/>` · `<app-contracts-contract-details-section [(rates)]="rates" [(matrix)]="matrix" [applications]="applications()" [commChannels]="commChannels()" [unitConversions]="rateCardRows()" [editable]="true"/>` · `<app-contracts-addons-section [(quotas)]="quotas" [(overageRates)]="overageRates" [availableChannels]="commChannels()" [editable]="true"/>`. Imported via the `../../components` barrel.
- **LOOKUPS + MATRIX SEED (donor `loadLookups`+`ensureRateMatrixDefaults` ported):** `effect()` on `accountId()` → `loadLookups(id)` forkJoins W1 `getApplicationOptions`+`getChannelOptions` (SOR-unwrapped), seeds `rateCardRows` via `createUnitConversionsForChannels`, then seeds `matrix` with first app+first channel via `createRateMatrixForSelection`. Effect also seeds `currency` from `walletStrategy().currency` (guarded against loop). `loadingLookups` gates steps 2-4 validity.
- **PER-STEP VALIDITY (donor predicates ported):** step2 = every rate-card row priceValue>=0; step3 = app+channel+ratingUnit set AND every matrix cell non-null>=0; step4 = addons OPTIONAL (empty=valid), any present quota/overage well-formed. `isCurrentStepValid` switches on `currentStep()` (+`!loadingLookups()` for 2-4 +`!saving()`). `isFormValid` = all 4.
- **SAVE→PAYLOAD (reaches POST):** `onFinish` → `assembleForm()` builds `ContractFormValue` (Step-1 strings→Dates via `toDate`; `...createEmptyContractForm(currency)` base + `unitConversions=rateCardRows()`, `rateMatrix=matrix()`, `rates=rates()`, `quotas`/`overageRates`) → `api.createContract(accountId, form)` (the W1 service's private `toCreatePayload(accountId,form)` → `toUpdatePayload` flattens matrix via `syncRateMatrixIntoRates`, filters null-rate cells, formats dates to local `YYYY-MM-DDT00:00:00`, drops server-assigned fields). Success → `(created)` emit + `contractsCostManagement.contractCreated` success toast (`FalconNotificationService.push({intent,title,subtitle})`); failure → `sor.errors[0]` via error toast (SOR already maps HTTP/transport errors).
- **LIST-PAGE WIRE:** `contracts-cost-management.component.{ts,html}` — imported `ContractsAddWizardComponent`; replaced the `mode()==='add'` dashed placeholder with `<app-contracts-add-wizard [accountId]="selectedAccountId()!" [accountName]="headerName()" [walletStrategy]="walletStrategy()" (created)="onContractCreated()" (cancelled)="onWizardCancel()"/>` (the `!` is safe — `add` is unreachable unless `hasSelectedNode()` + wallet strategy configured). New `onContractCreated()` → success toast + `resetToList()` + `loadSelectedAccount(selectedAccountId())` reload.
- **i18n (2 EDITS):** ADDED `contractsCostManagement.contractCreated` to en ("Contract created successfully") + ar ("تم إنشاء العقد بنجاح"). ALL other keys pre-existed (wizard.title/stepPrefix/nav.*, wizard.contractInformation.* incl committedValue/currency, button.{cancel,previous,next,save}, loadingLookups, validation.*, organizationFallback, addContract). Both JSON re-validated via JSON.parse.
- **DEVIATIONS:** (1) stepper is `falcon-angular-stepper` (mandated) NOT the donor's `DynamicStepperComponent`. (2) currency is a DISABLED single-SAR `falcon-angular-dropdown` per SoT CwStep3 (display-only, still drives the wire enum). (3) `onNavigationBlocked(_detail)` keeps the unused-param eslint warning to match the canonical templates-wizard consumer.

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — BUG-06 Add-Client Step 5 validation inconsistent with Step 1 — BUILD-GREEN
Status: 🟢 BUILD-GREEN admin `5d3e6c445e525541` (`nx build admin-console --skip-nx-cache`, exit 0, "Successfully ran target build for project admin-console and 6 tasks", 0 TS/template errors; only PRE-EXISTING repo-wide warnings = bundle-budget + ES2022-target + ~30 "unused in TS compilation" host-shell/libs files, NONE in my file/dir) · NO COMMITS · FE-only, 1 file, ZERO behavior change (behavior-preserving refactor) · NOT browser-verified (needs MF+Docker+Zitadel login + 5-step wizard nav to reach Step 5; verified by build + exhaustive code trace per brief).
- **BUG-06 root cause:** Step 5 (`client-account-owner-step.component.ts`) INJECTED its rule map `CLIENT_ACCOUNT_OWNER_VALIDATIONS` but IGNORED it — called validators directly via private `errMsg()`/bespoke `userError()` + a hand-rolled `isFormValid` with 6 direct validator calls. Step 1 routes every field through shared `fieldErrorMessage(value, field, rules, touched)` + gates Next with `allFieldsValid(value, rules)`. Different code path = the "validation inconsistent Step 1 vs 5" QA symptom.
- **FIX (refactor to Step-1 pattern):** `firstError/lastError/nidError/phoneError/emailError` → `fieldErrorMessage(this.value(), '<field>', this.rules, this.touched())`. `userError` → `fieldErrorMessage(...,'ownerUser',...) ?? this.usernameAsyncError()` (mirrors Step 1's `accountNameError` sync-then-async). `isFormValid` 6 direct calls → `if (!allFieldsValid(this.value(), this.rules)) return false;` + KEPT the 2 async gates (`usernameCheckPending()`, `usernameAsyncError()`) verbatim. Deleted dead private `errMsg()`.
- **PARITY = IDENTICAL (no rule-map additions needed, map was COMPLETE).** The rule map binds `[personNameValidator]`(first/last), `[userNameValidator]`(user), `[nationalIdValidator]`(nid), `[phoneValidator]`(phone), `[emailValidator]`(email) — these are `export const X: ValidatorFn = r.<rule>()` SINGLETONS in named-validators.ts = literally the SAME function objects the old direct calls invoked. `errMsg(field,fn,val)` and `fieldErrorMessage(value,field,rules,touched)` are provably identical for single-validator fields (same `messageFor(merged)`, same `hasLiveError`+touched gate, same `new FormControl(value[field])`). No password/confirm-password fields exist in Step 5 (ownerPwd = backend-gen read-only, no validator; no confirm field) so no gap there.
- **userError edge proven safe:** old `userError` returned null (NOT async) when sync `userNameValidator` gave a suppressed non-live error; only non-live key from `userName()` is `{required}` (empty username); async pipe returns null for empty/sync-invalid input → `?? usernameAsyncError()` = `?? null` = null. Same outcome. Async username-uniqueness pipeline (`toObservable→debounce(300)→isUserExist(username,email,phone)→toSignal`) + `FALCON_RESERVED_USERNAMES` check UNTOUCHED.
- **Imports:** removed `personNameValidator,nationalIdValidator,phoneValidator,emailValidator,hasLiveError,messageFor`; added `allFieldsValid,fieldErrorMessage`; KEPT `userNameValidator`+`FormControl` (async pipe still uses both). Build emitted ZERO unused-import warnings → confirms drop/keep correct.
- **File (1):** `apps/admin-console/src/app/features/org-hierarchy-page/components/wizard-components/add-client-wizard/client-account-owner-step/client-account-owner-step.component.ts`. NO validation lib, NO rule-map (`validations/validations.ts` unchanged — already complete), NO HTML, NO mgmt mirror (Add-Client is Falcon-only/admin-only).

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — BUG-15 Login OTP countdown timer not re-rendering (zoneless) + 120→60 fallback — BUILD-GREEN
Status: 🟢 BUILD-GREEN host-shell `bf9fdac34de5d048` (`nx build host-shell --skip-nx-cache`, exit 0, "Successfully ran target build for project host-shell and 5 tasks", 0 TS/template errors; only PRE-EXISTING unused-file + ES2022-target warnings, NONE in my files) · NO COMMITS · FE-only, host-shell only, 4 files · NOT browser-verified (needs running login→OTP flow = MF+Docker+Zitadel; verified by build + code trace per brief).
- **BUG-15 root cause:** app is ZONELESS (`provideZonelessChangeDetection()`). `enter-otp.component.ts startTimer()` ran `interval(1000).pipe(tap(...))` mutating PLAIN fields (`remainingSeconds`/`timerDisplay`/`screenState`/`totalSeconds`) with NO signal + NO markForCheck → an RxJS interval callback runs outside Angular CD with no zone to schedule a tick, so `{{ timerDisplay }}`, ring `stroke-dashoffset` (via `timerPercent` getter), Resend `[disabled]` (via `remainingSeconds`/`screenState`), and the Expired transition all FROZE on screen.
- **APPROACH CHOSEN = `ChangeDetectorRef.markForCheck()` (minimal alternative), NOT signals.** Why: timer state is read/mutated at ~13+ non-template sites (onOtpValueChange, resetOtpForRetry, onVerify, onResend, startTimer, success/expired subscribe branches, isOtpComplete/timerPercent getters, ~13 `screenState===` enum checks). Signal conversion = ~30 read/write edits/component + template + high risk of a missed `()` silently breaking logic. One `markForCheck()` at the END of each per-tick `tap` (the Expired transition is INSIDE that same tap → one call covers tick + expiry) re-renders every per-tick binding. Lowest blast radius. Matches file's own OnPush precedent (forgot-password already converted FORM state to signals for the identical "subscribe-mutation no-CD under OnPush" reason; timer left unfixed).
- **enter-otp** (`apps/host-shell/.../auth/enter-otp/enter-otp.component.ts`): +`ChangeDetectorRef` import +`private cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)` +`this.cdr.markForCheck()` at end of interval tap. NOTE: enter-otp does NOT declare OnPush, but ZONELESS means even default CD won't tick on RxJS interval without a CD trigger → fix needed regardless. NO template change.
- **forgot-password-flow AUDIT RESULT = SAME DEFECT, FIXED.** `forgot-password-flow.component.ts` IS `ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush` (line 58) and its timer (lines ~264-271) had the identical plain-field mutation in `tap` → same freeze. Applied the SAME `markForCheck()` fix (+import +inject +1 call). Its FORM state was already signal-based; only the timer was missed.
- **Secondary (120→60 fallback):** backend OTP TTL = Identity `appsettings.json Zitadel:Otp:ExpirySeconds=60` (returned as `otpExpiresInSeconds`). Changed all FE `?? 120` / bare-`120` OTP-duration defaults → 60 (API value still WINS when present): enter-otp `otpSeconds`/`totalSeconds` fields + `?? 120`→`?? 60` in ngOnInit; get-started OTP-pending `?? 120`→`?? 60` + pwd-change branch literal `120`→`60`; forgot-password 2× `?? 120`→`?? 60` + `totalSeconds`; AND `auth-flow-state.service.ts DEFAULT_STATE.otpConfig.seconds 120→60` (this seed is what `getOtpConfig()` returns when no temp session set → was the upstream 120 that made enter-otp's `?? 60` unreachable; now truly 60 everywhere).
- **Files (4):** `apps/host-shell/src/app/features/auth/enter-otp/enter-otp.component.ts` · `.../forgot-password-flow/forgot-password-flow.component.ts` · `.../get-started/get-started.component.ts` · `.../services/auth-flow-state.service.ts`. NO HTML changed (markForCheck approach). NO libs/backend/PES touched.
- **Regression note:** markForCheck only marks the component dirty per tick (no logic/state change) → no behavior change beyond the view now reflecting the already-correct field values; 120→60 only shifts the FALLBACK when API omits the value (real flow pipes the API value, unaffected). RUNTIME confirmation (number ticks, ring animates, 0→Expired+Resend-enabled, initial==otpExpiresInSeconds) PENDING running auth env.

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — BUG-14 Add-User limit error shows generic toast (not backend msg) — BUILD-GREEN both consoles
Status: 🟢 BUILD-GREEN admin `c79308f00b801004` + mgmt `978948a3d2329514` (`nx build … --skip-nx-cache`, both exit 0, 0 new errors/warnings vs green baseline admin `118b71f45f3cb250`/mgmt `bf591de2cd1af664`) · NO COMMITS · FE-only, 5 files · NOT browser-verified (no Docker/MF/login env; build+code-trace per brief).
- **BUG-14:** Add-User over-limit (and ALL backend business failures) showed static "Something went wrong / We could not complete the request" instead of the backend's localized message — unlike Add-Node "level limit" which surfaces it. ROOT: `createUserSubmit$` parked real errors in `wizardBackendErrors` then threw generic `new Error('[hierarchy] create user business validation failed')`; shared `falcon-wizard-finalization.showSubmitErrorToast()` ignored the caught error and always used static `errorToastBody()`; + i18n key `hierarchy.validation.normalUserLimitReached` was MISSING (BACKEND_ERROR_KEY['NormalUserLimitReached'] mapped to it).
- **NOTE — paths differ from brief:** both add-user-state.signals.ts live under `apps/{admin,management}-console/src/app/features/org-hierarchy-page/services/state/` (NOT `…/add-user-wizard/services/state/`). Both files BYTE-IDENTICAL → same Part-A edit applied to BOTH (mgmt has its own copy, confirmed).
- **Localized-message source (cited):** `mapBackendEnvelope` (models.ts:284-292) sets `errorMessages = r.errors` (backend's localized strings, same array Add-Node reads via `res.errorMessages?.[0]` at node-drawer-state.signals.ts:103). So `res.errorMessages?.[0]` / first-envelope `.message` IS localized. Fix prefers that; ONLY when the envelope carries a bare CODE (message==code OR message is itself a key in `BACKEND_ERROR_KEY`) does it `i18n.translate(keyForBackendCode(code))` → maps `NormalUserLimitReached`→new key. Imports `BACKEND_ERROR_KEY,keyForBackendCode,TranslateService` from `@falcon` (all re-exported via barrel; verified).

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — BUG-13 acc-owner main-node-only in add-user wizard (BOTH consoles) — BUILD-GREEN
Status: 🟢 BUILD-GREEN admin `619be606801ba7db` + mgmt (both `nx build … --skip-nx-cache` exit 0, 0 TS/template errors; only pre-existing unused-file + SignalR + bundle-budget warnings) · NO COMMITS · FE-only, 4 files, zero backend/lib/PES changes · NOT browser-verified (needs full MF+Docker+login; verified by build + code trace per brief).
- **BUG-13:** add-user role list offered "Account Owner" (acc-owner) on ANY non-Falcon-root node incl. sub-nodes. Rule: acc-owner is MAIN (tenant root) node only. Root cause = `roleOptionsForNode(nodeId)` had no main-vs-sub distinction (admin: returned `ACCOUNT_ROLE_OPTIONS` for every non-Falcon-root id; mgmt: ignored nodeId entirely, always returned all 3 acc roles).
- **Node accessor (cited):** wizard reads the selected node via `this.state.selectedNode()` → `HierarchyPageStateService.selectedNode` (delegates to `treeSlice.selectedNode`) → `ClientNode | null`. `ClientNode` has `type:'root'|'client'|'sub-node'` + `level?`. Depth via `mapGetNodeResponseToClientNode` (`org-hierarchy-page/models/models.ts`): `level 0→'root'`, `1→'client'`, `>=2→'sub-node'`. Admin tree: synthetic Falcon root=level0 'root' (id=FALCON_ROOT_NODE.id), tenant=level1 'client' (=MAIN), children level2+ 'sub-node'. **Mgmt tree: tenant root=level0 'root' (=MAIN, the Client's own node); EVERY descendant is level1 'client' or level2+ 'sub-node' (all sub-nodes).**
- **Fix:** changed `roleOptionsForNode(nodeId:string)` → `roleOptionsForNode(node:RoleScopeNode)` (RoleScopeNode = `Pick<ClientNode,'id'> & Partial<Pick<ClientNode,'type'|'level'>>`). Kept SYSTEM_/ACCOUNT_ROLE_OPTIONS as-is; just filter `acc-owner`. Admin: Falcon root (id match || type 'root')→SYSTEM; `isMainNode = type==='client'||level===1`→all ACCOUNT; else ACCOUNT minus owner. Mgmt: `isMainNode = type==='root'||level===0`→all ACCOUNT; else minus owner (NOTE: mgmt main=tenant-root-ONLY, so type-'client' level-1 direct children are treated as sub-nodes → correctly lose owner). Call sites pass `this.state.selectedNode() ?? {id:this.nodeId(),type:undefined,level:undefined}` (transient-null fallback yields owner-less account set — never over-grants owner).
- **Files (4):** admin `…/add-user-wizard/models/models.ts` + `…/add-user-wizard.component.ts` (line ~250); mgmt same two paths (call site line ~257-264, reused existing `selectedNode`/`isAccountRoot` block). Consolidated ClientNode into existing import (no dup-import lint).
- **BACKEND FLAG (out of scope, not implemented):** Identity `CreateUserProcess` should reject acc-owner creation under a sub-node as defense-in-depth (FE gate is bypassable — corroborated by 2026-05-28 Essentials finding that user-creation is FE-gate-only at the backend).

## 2026-05-29 — Ammar Core-Charging — Wallet-parity discovery: canonical backend wallet-business spec (READ-ONLY)
Status: 🔍 DISCOVERY-ONLY (no edits/commits/build/servers) · feature/realtime-failure-reason-alignment vs origin/main · report → `C:/Falcon/qa/runs/wallet-parity-2026-05-29/discovery/01-charging-backend.md`
Code-verified the SoT for wallet/transfer/balance business in falcon-core-charging-svc. KEY facts (all [CODE]-cited in report):
- **Model is OCS, not legacy Wallet/Ledger.** Aggregate `OcsWallet` (id=`{OWNERTYPE}:{ownerId}:{CHANNEL}:{CUR}`) with `buckets[]`; balance = Σ AvailableAmount over ContractFunded+Active+in-window buckets (GetAccountWalletsHandler.cs:143-154). Legacy Wallets/AccountWalletConfigs collections no longer touched. `eOcsWalletOwnerType{Account=1,User=2,Node=3}`.
- **canTransfer is NOT emitted by charging (DEFINITIVE answer to brain b-4).** `GetAccountWalletsResponse` has only ids+balances+master/channel/owner/sub — zero permission flags (GetAccountWalletsResponse.cs:3-38). Controller has only `[Authorize]`, no PES. So canTransfer is gateway/FE-computed; FE clause `&& data().canTransfer` is always undefined.
- **Transfer governance = `eUserType` (Falcon vs Client), NOT PES.** ResolveOcsTransferWalletsPolicy.cs: Multiple-wallet Master↔AccountChannel is Falcon-only; Client limited to AccountChannel↔OwnerChannel + OwnerChannel↔OwnerChannel (matching channel). Single-wallet = ALL↔ALL only, no Account→Account. Same-currency + source≠dest enforced. Throws `InvalidTransferWallets`/`WalletNotFound`/`WalletSettingsNotFound`/`InsufficientBalance`/`WalletVersionConflict`.
- **No FluentValidation** anywhere (imperative Validate() + FalconException guards). **No transfer-limit-% / no min-max** (only Amount>0). **Transfer is NOT idempotent server-side** (no idempotency key — unlike debit/reserve) → FE must guard double-submit.
- **BR-AM-26 matrix** = ResolveOwnerWalletProvisioningPolicy.cs: (UserBased,User)/(NodeBased,Node)→provision; Single→["ALL"], Multiple→per-visible-channel. Strategy read-model `WalletStrategyReadModel{BalanceOwnerType,WalletStructure,Currency}`.
- **Kafka:** consume commerce.{wallet-configured,user-wallet-create,subnode-wallet-create,comm-channel-shown,order-created}.v1; produce charging.{order-payment-processed,ocs-wallet-events}.v1 (transfer emits BalanceTransferred on ocs-wallet-events). Topics in appsettings.json:94-101.
- **main-vs-branch delta = order/do-payment failure-reason only (NOT transfer/get-wallets/strategy).** Committed: +`WalletNotConfigForTheNode=3`, new `WalletNotConfiguredException`, DeductFalconServiceCostHandler free-service(0)→Paid + missing-strategy→WalletNotConfig (not InsufficientFunds) + rethrow-unexpected, AllocateOcsMonetaryBucketsPolicy allows amount==0. Uncommitted working tree: DirectDebitHandler zero-amount short-circuit + tests + resx. Transfer/get-wallets business byte-identical to origin/main.

## 2026-05-28 — Ammar Core-Charging — RESEARCH (no code): wallet seed for test-tenant-001
Status: 🔍 RESEARCH-ONLY (no seed/code/migration written; deep code dive of Charging + Commerce + Core-Gateway + FE) · findings returned to parent agent
Deep-dived the wallet BACKEND to scope a per-node/per-role wallet seed for `test-tenant-001`. Key code-verified findings:
- **Charging uses the OCS model** (NOT the doc's legacy Wallet/Ledger). Collections (DB `FalconChargingDB`): `wallets` (OcsWallet, `walletId={OWNERTYPE}:{ownerId}:{CHANNEL}:{CUR}`, buckets[]), `wallet_strategy_read_model` (_id=accountId STRING), `wallet_balance_snapshots` (_id=walletId), + `wallet_ledger`/`ocs_reservations`/`contract_balance_summary` (read path doesn't need these). [CODE] OcsCollectionNames.cs, OcsWallet.cs.
- **Wallets key on `accountId`**, which is `OwnerType=Account` master `ACCOUNT:{acct}:ALL:SAR`; per-node wallets are `NODE:{nodeId}:...`. GetAccountWallets matches `OcsWallet.AccountId == accountId` exact. [CODE] MongoOcsRepositories.cs:26.
- **🔑 THE accountId for test-tenant-001 is the NODE _id hex `000000000000000000a11001`, NOT the tenantId string** — because the mgmt page calls Core-Gateway `accounts/{id}/hierarchy` → gateway passes JWT tenantId to Commerce `accounts/hierarchy?accountId={tenantId}` → Commerce `GetAccountHierarchyHandler` returns `AccountId = accountNode.Id` (the node _id) and only THEN calls Charging `get-account-wallets` with that node-id. [CODE] GetAccountHierarchyEndpoint.cs:44,95; GetAccountHierarchyHandler.cs:91.
- **🔴 BLOCKER B-W1**: Commerce `n.Id == tenantId("test-tenant-001")` on a `[BsonRepresentation(ObjectId)]` Id with a NON-HEX string → driver FormatException at query render → wallet hierarchy 500s for test-tenant-001 UNLESS resolved. Brand accts dodge this (tenantId IS hex).
- **🔴 BLOCKER B-W2**: gateway calls Charging ONLY when `!CanSave`; `CanSave = (Settings.WalletSettings is null)` keyed on `OwnerId==tenantId`. Existing seed's TT Settings doc has NO `walletSettings` → CanSave=true → balances never fetched. Seed MUST add Commerce `Settings.walletSettings={currency,walletBalanceType,walletType}` for ownerId=tenantId. [CODE] GetAccountHierarchyHandler.cs:56-64; WalletConfiguration.cs.
- **Transfer limit % is NOT enforced in Charging at all** — `balanceTransferLimitPercentage` lives only in Commerce Settings + FE; `TransferBalanceHandler` validates path/currency/sufficiency only; gateway mapper doesn't even populate `balanceTransferLimitPct`. So "over-limit" can't be exercised via Charging today (FE-only/unwired). [CODE] TransferBalanceHandler.cs, AccountHierarchyMapper.cs.
- **Wallet PES = all `sys.*` (Falcon-only): wallet-strategy.view/edit, master-wallet.view, wallet.transfer** — silent-deny for ALL 3 client roles. Per-role wallet access is FE-only (server-driven canSave/canTransfer, not data). [BRAIN-OUT] acc-{owner,admin,user}.capability.md:85-88.
- **Funding decision tree** (do-payment outcomes, exercised from service-pricing not the wallet page): master≥amt→Master; master>0 & master+comm≥amt→Both(if priorities) else Fail(CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired); comm≥amt→CommChannel(if priorities) else Fail(CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired); else Fail(InsufficientFunds). Comm-channel wallets must be `OwnerType=Account` (not Node). [CODE] ResolveWalletFundingDecisionPolicy.cs; DirectDebitHandler.cs:479.
- Existing brand seed mechanics (extend SAME pattern): `upsertWalletStrategy(accId,structure)` + `upsertOcsWallet(accId,ownerType,ownerId,channel,buckets)` (deleteOne+insert idempotent) + auto snapshot. seed-service-scenarios.js:680-822.

Id` with **NO `[BsonRepresentation(BsonType.ObjectId)]`** (unlike every other entity Id). `GetMainNodeInfoHandler.cs:23` projects `AccountId = n.AccountDetails.Id`; depending on how the doc was written Mongo can hand it back as a raw ObjectId → serializes as `{$oid}` obj / legacy `{timestamp,machine,...}` obj / 12-byte array, NOT clean hex. ## 2026-05-28 — Ammar Essentials — PES verify+runtime-exercise for 3 Client roles (test-tenant-001) — focus user-creation
Status: 🟢 RUNTIME-VERIFIED (live 18-container Docker stack, real JWTs through real PES) · NO seed edits needed · NO COMMITS
Verified — did NOT guess — the PES integration for `test-tenant-001` against the SoT `BuiltInRoleCatalog.cs` + authority dataset, then runtime-exercised all 3 canonical roles.
- **Sources of truth read**: `falcon-core-access-svc/src/T2.PES/Authorization/BuiltInRoleCatalog.cs` (the catalog the provisioner emits) · FE `falcon-access.registry.ts` (Add-User uses TWO keys: `accountUser.add`→`acc.account-user` and `orgUser.add`→`acc.org-user`) · authority dataset `05-capability-maps/*` + `03-pes-keys/REGISTRY-RAW.md`.
- **Add-User PES model (authoritative, NOT owner+admin-both as the brief framed it)**: `acc.account-user.add` (ROOT add-user) = acc-owner ALLOW, acc-admin **silent-deny (NO rule in catalog)**, acc-user explicit DENY. `acc.org-user.add` (SUB-NODE add-user) = owner ALLOW, admin ALLOW, user DENY. FE tree `canAddUserForNode` = `isRootSelection ? canAddAccountUser : canAddOrgUser` → at root only owner sees Add-User; at a sub-node owner+admin do. [authority `acc-admin.capability.md:110`]
- **3-way diff (catalog vs `pes-account-role-rules.json` template vs LIVE PES) via Node script**: for EVERY brief-scoped resource (hierarchy/org/account-user/org-user/users/settings/account/profile/pwd-sec/ips/quota/services/contract/contact-group/app-entry) → **0 missing, 0 conflicts, 0 extras**. Template+live each carry 31/29/32 p-rules (owner/admin/user); catalog has 40/38/41 — the delta is EXACTLY the 9/9/9 `acc.wallet-balance` + `transfer-*` rules, which are OUT of this brief's feature scope (wallet has its own seed path). **So NO edit to `pes-account-role-rules.json` was required — the data was already complete + correct.** Did NOT add wallet rules (out of scope).
- **Links**: all **7/7** g-rules present + correctly oriented (`g, sub=r:<role>@tenant, obj=u:<zitadelId>@tenant`, action=all, effect=allow): 1 owner (accowner) + 3 admin (accadmin/accadmin-hr/accadmin-db) + 3 user (accuser/accuser-cc/accuser-care). JWT.sub of each canonical login maps to the right role g-rule. No link fix needed.
- **Live PES decisions** (`POST :5296/pes/authorize/resources`, built EXACTLY like FE `access-control.facade.ts` — `sub.kind="u:<sub>@tenant"`, no roles array, response key `<kind>_<seqNo>` w/ bare-`<kind>` fallback when seqNo=0): matrix matches catalog 100%. owner=ALLOW on everything; admin=ALLOW hierarchy/org-add/org-user-add/users/settings/account-edit/contact-group but explicit-DENY account-user-add/ips/quota/services/contract/profile-edit; user=DENY all except contact-group.view. **jq gotcha**: `false // x` falls through (jq treats false as empty) → use `has()` not `//` to read DENY vs absent.
- **🔑 BIGGEST RUNTIME FINDING — user-creation is NOT PES-gated at the backend; the Add-User PES gate is FRONT-END-ONLY.** `POST identity/user` via core-gateway `:7038` with acc-user's JWT placing a user at root a11001 → **HTTP 200 isSuccessful:true** (NOT 403). Same for owner+admin. All 3 created users landed at a11001 (Mongo: nodeId=a11001, tenantId=test-tenant-001, role=6, status=Pending). ROOT CAUSE [CODE]: identity `CreateUserEndpoint`+`CreateUserProcess` have ZERO PES check (only `RequireAuthorization()` + role-validity + quota + dup-username + tenant/node-context); gateway `identity-proxy` route uses `AuthorizationPolicy:"ClientOnly"` = `RequireClaim(UserType==Client)` ONLY (all 3 acc-* are Client) — no per-resource PES policy. So acc-user is denied ONLY by the FE hiding the button. acc-admin also succeeded creating at SUB-NODE HR(a11002) → HTTP 200, landed at a11002. Quota didn't bite (tenant has no Settingss doc → MaxNormalUserLimit null → unlimited). **This is a real backend authz gap worth a flag.**
- **Cleanup**: all 4 throwaway test users (3@root + 1@subnode) soft-deleted in Mongo + deactivated in Zitadel + their auto-created PES g-links DELETEd (`DELETE :5296/pes/policyrule` by rule id). g-rule count restored 7→10→7. State pristine.
- **Final**: all 7 seeded users (3 canonical + 4 distributed) login `Admin@1234` → isSuccessful=true stage=4 JWT. All 3 client types work as expected per the authority dataset. ## 2026-05-28 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — B-13 commerce/Setting 404 TranslateService warning fix
Status: 🟢 BROWSER-VERIFIED (live accowner E2E vs Docker) · BUILD+LINT GREEN · NO COMMITS · FE-only
Fixed the cosmetic `[TranslateService] Translation key not found: "Settings not found"` warning on the mgmt-console Org Hierarchy page (Settings tab path).
- **Root cause** (single leak point): `commerce/Setting?ownerId=<nodeId>` → HTTP 404 `{errorCodes:['SettingsNotFound'],errorMessages:['Settings not found']}` → `SettingsService.getSettings()` catchError → `httpFailure()` → `extractServerError()` (models.ts:250-258) keeps raw English `errorMessages[0]` (DISCARDS errorCodes+status) → slice `firstMessage()` (settings-tab.signals.ts:304-313) calls `i18n.translate("Settings not found")` → warning at [CODE] `libs/falcon/src/language/lib/services/translate.service.ts:218`.
- **Decision = Option (a) swallow-to-empty-state** (NOT errorCode→i18n). Surveyed app: dominant `*NotFound`/failed-read pattern is SWALLOW into benign value — `HierarchyService.getTree()` services.ts:118-130, `loadNodeChildren()` :141-155, `getUsers()` :188-198, info-panel `resetToEmpty()`. ZERO existing errorCode→i18n convention; en.json has no `SettingsNotFound` key; only code-map is `CODE_TO_STATUS` (code→HTTP#, write-validation only).
- **Fix** (1 file, localized): `apps/management-console/src/app/features/org-hierarchy-page/components/tab-components/settings-tab/services/settings.service.ts` — added private `isSettingsNotFound(err)` predicate (status===404 OR error.errorCodes includes 'SettingsNotFound') + getSettings() catchError now returns `of<ServiceOperationResult<SettingsViewModel>>({isSuccessful:true, result: fromGetSettingsResponse(null)})` for that case → slice success branch → empty editable defaults, NEVER hits firstMessage()/translate(). All other failures unchanged → httpFailure(). NO i18n keys added, NO backend change.
- **Gotcha** (TS2769): a bare `of({...} as ServiceOperationResult<...>)` breaks `of` overload resolution — use the explicit generic `of<T>({...})` form. Cost ~5 build iterations.
- **Verify (static)**: `nx build management-console` GREEN (hash e97dbbaefd2b545a), `eslint settings.service.ts` exit 0, predicate matrix 7/7 PASS via node (404+code/404/code→true; 500/401/network/null→false). Served chunk `…org-hierarchy-page_routes_ts.js` on live :4301 contains `SettingsNotFound` ×6 = my code is what runs.
- **Verify (LIVE BROWSER E2E)**: Docker WAS up (engine 29.4.3 via WSL2 despite `com.docker.service`=Stopped; commerce/core-gateway:7038/zitadel:8080 healthy 16h). Via Claude Preview MCP (host-shell :4200 + standalone mgmt :4301 = my rebuilt code): accowner/Admin@1234 → **NO OTP** (`requiresOtp:false stage:4`, identity log "authenticated password-only, no OTP") → authed shell → `#/management-console/organization-hierarchy`. Tree rendered (Test Tenant 001 + 5 children + Contact Center lazy-expand → Inbound/Outbound/Customer Care via `commerce/Node?NodeId=…a11004`), 52 falcon-* els. Selected root → Settings tab. With console.warn/error hooks installed BEFORE the cycle: `translateWarnings:[]`, `settingsNotFoundWarning:[]`, `totalWarns:0`, `errorsCaptured:[]` across login→tree→Setting-call→select→lazy-expand. Settings body renders success empty-state (passwordSecurity/allowedIps/accountLimitations `offsetParent`-VISIBLE; error banner `errorBanner_VISIBLE:false`). Screenshot captured.
- **IMPORTANT NUANCE**: for accowner's ROOT node a11001 the live `commerce/Setting` now returns **HTTP 200** (real Advanced+3IPs+quota), NOT 404 — because Ammar-Essentials seeded the Setting doc earlier today (see their 2026-05-28 "Settings-404 fix" log entry: seed BOTH ownerId=tenantId for GET + ownerId=nodeId for PUT). So the original 404 no longer reproduces for THIS node. My FE fix is still correct + proven harmless (success path renders clean, zero regression) and is the durable guard for any node genuinely lacking a Setting record (sub-nodes / fresh accounts) — that path is proven by the 7/7 predicate matrix. The warning bug is now defended at BOTH layers (seed provides data; FE swallows 404 if absent).
- **GOTCHA (verify-methodology, for future agents)**: (1) `body.innerText` regex FALSELY matches the inactive `@else if(isError())` Angular template text → to assert a branch is NOT rendered, check element visibility (`offsetParent!==null`), never innerText. (2) Claude Preview MCP tools need a REAL `serverId` from `preview_start`/`preview_list`; calling them with a guessed id silently fails every call (I wasted a batch doing this) — and the MCP uses its OWN launch.json (server names are `falcon-`prefixed: `falcon-host-shell`/`falcon-management-console`), NOT the workspace `.claude/launch.json`. `falcon-management-console` failed to spawn (node ENOENT) but `falcon-host-shell` + a standalone mgmt http-server on :4301 worked.
- **New gap (NOT fixed — spawned background task)**: identical latent translate-leak in `firstMessage()` of `info-panel-state.signals.ts:402-411` (`commerce/information` path). For accowner it returns data so no leak observed; latent only. Spawned a task to apply the same swallow pattern to `InformationService.getInformation()`.

## 2026-05-28 — Ammar Essentials — test-tenant org node enriched (image + sub-node hierarchy)
Status: 🟢 LIVE-API-VERIFIED (full stack) · NO COMMITS · seed-only, no recompile
Enriched `ensureTestTenantNode()` in `Falcon/Falcon/Falcon/falcon-essentials/seed/seed-service-scenarios.js` so the canonical `test-tenant-001` accowner org tree shows an IMAGE + a 2-level child hierarchy (was a bare image-less, child-less leaf).
- **Image**: set `accountDetails.profilePictureUrl` on Main node `000000000000000000a11001` to an 83-byte valid teal PNG via `BinData(0, <b64>)`. Source-verified contract: `GetOrgHierarchyNodeHandler.GetNodeInfoAsync` sets `Url = node.AccountDetails?.ProfilePicture` (a `byte[]`), AutoMapper runs `FromBytesToImgSrc()` → magic-byte detect → `data:image/png;base64,...`. Must store raw PNG BYTES, not a string URL. Idempotent back-fill `$set`s the picture only when absent (the live node pre-existed without it).
- **Children**: 8 sub-nodes upserted on deterministic `_id`s `…a11002`–`…a11009`. 5 direct (level 2): Human Resources `a11002`, Digital Banking `a11003`, Contact Center `a11004` (hasChildren), Marketing `a11005`, IT & Cybersecurity `a11006`. 3 grandchildren under Contact Center (level 3): Inbound Call `a11007`, Outbound Call `a11008`, Customer Care `a11009`.
- **KEY SCHEMA FACTS** (so future seeds don't guess): (1) `Node.ParentId` is `[BsonRepresentation(BsonType.ObjectId)]` → `parentId` MUST be written as `ObjectId(...)`, NOT a string, or the node is INVISIBLE to `NodeAggregator.GetChildrenHierarchyInfoAsync` (`$lookup _id↔parentId` + `Eq(n=>n.ParentId,id)`). (2) `HasChildren` is COMPUTED (`AnyAsync(c.ParentId==id)` / `$lookup relatedNodeIds.Any()`), NOT a stored field — real child docs are the truth. (3) `path` = dot-joined node-id chain (`parentPath + "." + childId`); `GetOrgHierarchyNodeHandler.EnsureNodeInScopeAsync` authorises a Client child only when its `Path` startsWith the user's node Path — every descendant path is prefixed by the root id, so accowner is authorised through every level. (4) sub-nodes carry NO `accountDetails` (matches handler-created sub-nodes) → children show initials chip, root shows the image.
- **LIVE VERIFY** (accowner JWT `Admin@1234` → `POST :7777/api/auth/login` stage=4 → GET via core-gateway `:7038`): `GET /commerce/Node` → 1 root, `hasChildren=true`, `url=data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0K…` (len 134). `GET /commerce/Node?NodeId=…a11001` → 5 direct children. `GET /commerce/Node?NodeId=…a11004` → 3 grandchildren. Re-ran seed twice → stable at 9 nodes, 0 duplicate names, image set once. Stack: 18 containers up (comm-realtime unhealthy, irrelevant here).
- No frontend touched. No git commits. No .NET recompile. File copied into container at `/tmp/seed-service-scenarios.js` for the run (container has no `/seed` mount currently — copy with `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` to stop Git-Bash path mangling).

## Wave 4 SignalR delivery — FRONTEND side (2026-05-19, ammar-web-platform-ui)
- **Goal met:** do-payment order-status POLLING replaced with the SignalR PUSH from the
  gateway hub. Repo `falcon-web-platform-ui`, branch `polishing-v0.4`. NOT committed.
  Build-verified only — NOT runtime-tested (no live hub/browser exercised).
- **Dep:** added `@microsoft/signalr` `^8.0.17` to root `package.json` (Angular 21 / TS 5.9
  compatible; framework-agnostic). MF default share branch covers it (singleton lazy).
- **New transport service:** `apps/host-shell/src/app/core/realtime/order-status-realtime.service.ts`
  — `providedIn:'root'`, lazy single shared `HubConnection` to `{gateway}/hubs/order-status`,
  `withAutomaticReconnect()`, `accessTokenFactory` = `FALCON_AUTH.getAccessToken()`.
  Gateway URL resolved exactly like `RuntimeBaseUrlInterceptor` (APP_DEFAULT_GATEWAY →
  session-userType fallback). API: `joinOrder` / `leaveOrder` / `onOrderFinalized(orderId,cb)`
  returning unsubscribe; global `"OrderFinalized"` handler dispatches by `payload.orderId`.
- **Rewired `do-payment-priority-popup.component.ts`:** poll loop removed (`SimplePollService`,
  `pollSettled`, `beginPoll`, `handleTimeout`, `finalize` import gone). New flow: join hub +
  subscribe push → catch-up `getOrderStatus` GET once → bounded fallback GET after
  `environment.orderStatus.pollTimeoutMs` if no push. Push `eOrderStatus` translated to
  `ProcessState` (2→3, 3→4, 1→1) before the UNCHANGED `handleTerminal`. A `Failed` push
  triggers a reconciliation GET because the push omits `walletType` that `handleTerminal`
  needs for the `CommChannelPriorityOrderRequired` branch. `OrderStatusService` kept.
- **`SimplePollService`** left in the `@falcon` lib (generic util; only consumer was this
  component; deleting it was out of scope).
- **Builds:** `nx build` host-shell + admin-console + management-console all GREEN
  (dev config, `--skip-nx-cache`). New service file is lint-clean.

## Wave 4 SignalR delivery — System Gateway side (2026-05-19, ammar-system-gateway)
- **Goal met:** admin-console now learns a do-payment Order's terminal outcome via SignalR
  push instead of polling. System Gateway gained its FIRST Kafka consumer + a SignalR hub.
- **Repo:** `falcon-int-system-gateway-svc`, branch `main`. NOT committed (orchestrator
  builds in-container + commits on `polishing-v0.4`). Clean build verified locally (0/0).
- **SignalR hub:** `OrderStatusHub` at route `/hubs/order-status`, `[Authorize(FalconOnly)]`.
  Methods `JoinOrder(orderId)` / `LeaveOrder(orderId)` → group `order:{orderId}`.
- **Kafka consumer:** `OrderFinalizedConsumer` (BackgroundService) consumes Avro topic
  `commerce.order-finalized.v1`; on event → `IHubContext<OrderStatusHub>` push
  `"OrderFinalized"` to group `order:{orderId}`, payload `{orderId,status,failureReason}`
  camelCase (status/failureReason are Commerce enum INTs forwarded verbatim). Resilient:
  deserialize/push failures log + continue, never crash.
- **Avro:** `OrderFinalizedEvent` + nested `EventContext` mirror Commerce's schema exactly
  (namespace `Falcon.Commerce.Events`); reflection-based `AvroEventBase` (copied from Core
  Gateway pattern). Packages added: Confluent.Kafka/SchemaRegistry/Serdes.Avro 2.13.0.
- **JWT:** SignalR handshake token read from `access_token` query string for `/hubs/*` via
  `JwtBearerEvents.OnMessageReceived` in `ZitadelExtensions.cs` — reuses existing Zitadel auth.
- **Config:** new `GatewaySettings:Kafka` section. appsettings.json = in-container
  (`kafka:9092`, `http://schema-registry:8081`); Development = localhost (`localhost:9092`,
  `http://localhost:8085`). Consumer group `system-gateway-service`.
- **Files:** see backup `backups/2026-05-19-ammar-system-gateway-wave4-signalr.md`.

## Wave 3 edit-price/do-payment revamp — Commerce (2026-05-19, ammar-core-commerce)
- Repo `falcon-core-commerce-svc`, branch main, NOT committed. 3 items implemented:
  - **B1 idempotency:** duplicate `charging.order-payment-processed.v1` for terminal order no longer
    loops. `UpdatePendingFalconServiceOrderHandler` splits missing-order (throws OrderNotFound) vs
    already-terminal (no-op success + `AlreadyTerminal` flag); consumer treats true OrderNotFound as
    poison message (commit+warn). `CompleteFalconServicePaymentProcess` skips activation on replay.
  - **B3 auth:** added class-level `[Authorize]` to `NodeController` (it had none). Do-payment
    endpoints stay authenticated-only (Falcon + Account Owner per BRD); price/visibility keep FalconOnly.
  - **commerce.order-finalized.v1:** new POCO + Avro record + publisher + DI + topic; emitted from
    `CompleteFalconServicePaymentProcess` after order durably terminal, gated on `!AlreadyTerminal`.
- BUILD NOT VERIFIED — env has only .NET SDK 9.0.201, projects target net10.0 (NETSDK1045). Static
  verification only; not runtime-tested. Full detail: backups/2026-05-19-ammar-core-commerce-wave3-*.md

## Service-tables shadow-save + visibility bug-fix (2026-05-19, ammar-web-platform-ui)
- **Bug 1 (shadow Save no PUT) — root cause = LEADS A + B (both real).**
  - LEAD B (library): `falcon-data-table.component.ts` `onShadowAction` case 'save'
    unconditionally `setShadowMode(...,'view')` after emitting `shadowRowSave` → on
    validation-abort the edit form was torn down + the inline error (only in the
    'edit' branch) became invisible. FIX: removed the auto-flip; consumer owns mode.
  - LEAD A: `openShadowEdit` seeded `effectiveDate = today+10` → fails the periodic
    rule (renewDate.Day-1) for Monthly/Yearly rows. FIX: new `defaultEffectiveDateIso()`
    helper seeds the first submittable date. LEAD C = NOT a cause (ids match).

  .Operations.cs:122-129) does NOT clear `NewPricingInfo` — pending changes survive a
  hide. FIX: `shadowRowMap()` skips `!row.visible` rows; `collapseShadowState()` resets
  the row's shadow/edit state optimistically on hide click.

- **Status:** Completed — reused existing seed user `bmw.owner@bmw.local` (Mongo `_id 69ee2b6a36db66867f44ba89`, Zitadel id `370307597791657994`, BMW root node `69dd0985292aa01889681c57`, role=4/AccountOwner, userType=2, status=2, isEmailVerified=true, isPhoneVerified=true, seededBy `bmw-cars-seeder-2026-04-26`). Two writes only, zero container restarts, zero docker-compose edits. (1) Zitadel `POST /v2/users/370307597791657994/password` with `changeRequired=false` rotated password to `BmwOwner@1!`. (2) Mongo `FalconIdentityDb.Users` `_id` converted from raw string `"69ee2b6a36db66867f44ba89"` to `ObjectId("69ee2b6a36db66867f44ba89")` via deleteOne + insertOne (Mongo disallows `_id` updates) — same pattern as the 2026-04-26 PM-tenant fixup. **Why the type-conversion was needed:** `Falcon.Identity.Api/Domain/Entities/User.cs` line 11 has `[BsonRepresentation(BsonType.ObjectId)]` on `Id`, so the C# Mongo driver serializes `u.Id == "<hex>"` as `{_id: ObjectId(...)}`; the bmw-cars-seeder script had inserted raw-string `_id`s that the driver could never match, which is why `/user/me` returned 404 even though the JWT carried the correct `user-id` metadata claim. PES Type-103 unaffected (it indexes off Zitadel id `370307597791657994`, not Mongo `_id`). Final verification: `POST /api/auth/login {bmw.owner@bmw.local, BmwOwner@1!}` -> 200 stage 4 (no OTP, no password change required); `GET /api/user/me` with the access token -> 200 returning `{"id":"69ee2b6a36db66867f44ba89","nodeId":"69dd0985292aa01889681c57","tenantId":"69dd0985292aa01889681c57","role":4,"roleKey":"acc-owner","userType":2,"status":2,"isEmailVerified":true,"isPhoneVerified":true,...}`. The single source the new `RoleService` reads (`/user/me.roleKey`) returns `"acc-owner"` cleanly. Reusable across container restarts because all writes are persistent (Mongo volume + Zitadel postgres password hash). Backup written to `memory/backups/2026-04-28-ammar-essentials-bmw-owner-acc-owner.md`.
- **Notes:** Initial `/user/me` probe returned 404 with token valid, which surfaced the `_id`-shape mismatch in `bmw-cars-seeder`'s 9 BMW users (bmw.owner/bmw.admin/bmw.member + m3/m4/m5 admin+user pairs all have raw-string `_id`). Only `bmw.owner` was repaired in this session (minimum scope per the brief). The other 8 will silently fail any endpoint that uses `IRepository<User>.GetAsync(u => u.Id == currentUser.UserId)` (which is most user-detail endpoints) until they get the same delete+insert ObjectId conversion. Recommend a follow-up task: a one-shot mongosh script that converts all bmw-cars-seeder users in one pass, idempotent because `typeof _id !== "string"` skips after first run. Confirmed `Program.cs:20` sets `JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase` so the wire field is exactly `roleKey` (lowercase camel). Confirmed `UserMapper.MapToResponse` line 27 populates `RoleKey = UserRolePolicy.GetRoleKey(user.Role)` and `UserResponse.RoleKey` is on the contract (line 16) — the FE `RoleService` refactor is **not** broken; the `/user/me` endpoint correctly emits the field.

Recreated only `web-platform-ui` via `docker compose --profile frontend up -d --force-recreate --no-deps web-platform-ui`. Smoke test all green: `curl -sf http://localhost:4200/` 200, `http://localhost:4204/` 200 + `/remoteEntry.mjs` 200, `http://localhost:4301/` 200 + `/remoteEntry.mjs` 200. Container memory stabilised around 1.07 GiB after compile (vs. 1.5 GB old cap that OOMed). Docker Desktop has 15.08 GiB total — plenty of headroom; user does not need to bump Settings -> Resources. No commits, no push (per project rules); user's other uncommitted edits to `docker-compose.yml` (frontend profile gating, OTP off, etc.) untouched.
- **Notes:** Brief premise that one container "tries to serve host-shell + admin-console + management-console simultaneously" is partially correct: `npm run start` resolves to `nx serve host-shell` which boots **module federation dev-server with 2 remotes** (admin-console + management-console) via static-remote proxies — it does spawn child compile pipelines for the remotes inside the same Node process tree, which is what blew past 1.5 GB. The standalone `admin-console` (4204) + `management-console` (4301) containers were already up at 1.9 GiB each (separate processes with their own 2536 MB heap), so they served the 200s for those ports. Bumping host-shell to 8 GB resolved the OOM cleanly. Only one compile warning surfaced and it is pre-existing (Angular CLI ES2022/useDefineForClassFields advisory in tsconfig). No source files changed, only `C:\falcon\Falcon\docker-compose.yml`.

## 2026-05-31 — Ammar Web-Platform-UI — Profile-uploader bug-fix pass (branch polishing-v0.4)
Status: DONE, build-green ×4, NO COMMITS. Visual smoke deferred to user (no browser here).

### What was done (4 user-reported issues, all 6 `<falcon-angular-image-uploader>` instances)
- **FIX A (shared, 1 file):** `libs/falcon-ui-core/src/tailwind/file-uploader-tailwind-classes.ts` `fuTwWaveClasses()` — the two comma-bearing `animate-[fuWave_var(--…,4s)…]` / `…var(--…,6.4s)…_reverse]` utilities were SILENTLY DROPPED by Tailwind v4 (comma inside var() in an @source-scanned literal) → static water. Replaced with comma-free LITERAL durations `animate-[fuWave_4s_linear_infinite]` + `animate-[fuWave_6.4s_linear_infinite_reverse]` (= token defaults). `@keyframes fuWave` already global in falcon-ui-tokens. Kept `motion-reduce:animate-none`. Laser class already comma-free literal (untouched; not used by these consumers). ALSO fixes Templates' document/image uploader waves (same bug, beneficial).
- **FIX B (per 6 consumers):**
  1. Settled branch `status:'queued'` (→ aggregate 0% → water recedes) → `status:'success', progress:100` (→ aggregate 100% → teal water FILLS + STAYS).
  2. Renamed boolean `photoOversize`/`ownerPhotoOversize` → descriptor signal `photoError`/`ownerPhotoError: signal<FalconFileUploaderFile|null>(null)`. `(fileAdd)` handler: on `detail.file.status==='error'` → `photoError.set(detail.file)` (NOT photo/photoData — not accepted) + `uploadAnim.reset()`; else `photoError.set(null)` then accept+start. Preview computed returns `[photoError()!]` FIRST → uploader renders `is-error` → full RED water. `(fileRemove)`/clear → `photoError.set(null)`. Red "too large" line gated on `photoError()` (was the boolean). `aggregateStatus` maps error→progress 100 → full red water = the wanted red error status.
  3+4. `[maxSizeMB]="1"` + `[showWaves]="true"` + `[showBanner/showStatusBadge/showSuccessRing]="false"` were ALREADY present on all 6 — verified, no change. Backend/wire (splitDataUrl / parseDataUrl / profilePictureInfo / accountOwnerProfilePictureInfo) UNCHANGED.
